const escapeText=s=&gt;String(s).replace(/[&amp;&lt;&gt;&quot;&#x27;]/g,c=&gt;({&#x27;&amp;&#x27;:&#x27;&amp;amp;&#x27;,&#x27;&lt;&#x27;:&#x27;&amp;lt;&#x27;,&#x27;&gt;&#x27;:&#x27;&amp;gt;&#x27;,&#x27;&quot;&#x27;:&#x27;&amp;quot;&#x27;,&quot;&#x27;&quot;:&#x27;&amp;#39;&#x27;}[c]));
const same=(a,b)=&gt;Array.isArray(a)&amp;&amp;a.length===b.length&amp;&amp;b.every(v=&gt;a.includes(v));
try{const raw=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(KEY)||&#x27;{}&#x27;);if(raw&amp;&amp;typeof raw===&#x27;object&#x27;&amp;&amp;!Array.isArray(raw))for(const u of DATA){const h=raw[u.n];if(h&amp;&amp;Number.isInteger(h.score)&amp;&amp;h.score&gt;=0&amp;&amp;h.score&lt;=u.questions.length)history[u.n]=h}}catch(e){storageOK=false}
function save(){try{localStorage.setItem(KEY,JSON.stringify(history));storageOK=true}catch(e){storageOK=false}}
function applyRuby(off){document.body.classList.toggle(&#x27;no-ruby&#x27;,off);const b=document.getElementById(&#x27;rubyToggle&#x27;);b.textContent=&#x27;ふりがな：&#x27;+(off?&#x27;なし&#x27;:&#x27;あり&#x27;);b.setAttribute(&#x27;aria-pressed&#x27;,String(!off))}
try{applyRuby(localStorage.getItem(RUBY_KEY)===&#x27;off&#x27;)}catch(e){}
document.getElementById(&#x27;rubyToggle&#x27;).onclick=()=&gt;{const off=!document.body.classList.contains(&#x27;no-ruby&#x27;);applyRuby(off);try{localStorage.setItem(RUBY_KEY,off?&#x27;off&#x27;:&#x27;on&#x27;)}catch(e){}};
function selectDomain(value){domain=value;query=&#x27;&#x27;;home()}
function home(){
 unit=null;document.body.dataset.domain=domain;
 app.innerHTML=`&lt;div class=&quot;brand&quot;&gt;STEP BY STEP&lt;/div&gt;&lt;h1&gt;ひとつずつ、&lt;ruby&gt;確&lt;rt&gt;たし&lt;/rt&gt;&lt;/ruby&gt;かな&lt;ruby&gt;力&lt;rt&gt;ちから&lt;/rt&gt;&lt;/ruby&gt;に。&lt;/h1&gt;
 &lt;p class=&quot;muted&quot;&gt;${r(&#x27;領域&#x27;)}と${r(&#x27;単元&#x27;)}をえらんで、はじめましょう。&lt;br&gt;1${r(&#x27;単元&#x27;)}12${r(&#x27;問&#x27;)}。こたえると、すぐに${r(&#x27;解説&#x27;)}がわかります。&lt;/p&gt;
 &lt;div class=&quot;overview&quot;&gt;&lt;span&gt;33${r(&#x27;単元&#x27;)} · 396${r(&#x27;問&#x27;)}&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;${r(&#x27;学習済み&#x27;)} ${Object.keys(history).length} / 33${r(&#x27;単元&#x27;)}&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
 &lt;div class=&quot;tabs&quot; aria-label=&quot;領域を選ぶ&quot;&gt;${domains.map((d,i)=&gt;`&lt;button data-domain=&quot;${i}&quot; class=&quot;tab ${domain===i?&#x27;active&#x27;:&#x27;&#x27;}&quot; aria-pressed=&quot;${domain===i}&quot; onclick=&quot;selectDomain(${i})&quot;&gt;${d}&lt;small&gt;${counts[i]} ${r(&#x27;単元&#x27;)} · ${counts[i]*12}${r(&#x27;問&#x27;)}&lt;/small&gt;&lt;/button&gt;`).join(&#x27;&#x27;)}&lt;/div&gt;
 &lt;div class=&quot;toolbar&quot;&gt;&lt;h2&gt;${domains[domain]}&lt;/h2&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;filters&quot;&gt;&lt;input id=&quot;search&quot; type=&quot;search&quot; aria-label=&quot;単元をさがす&quot; placeholder=&quot;単元名・番号でさがす&quot; value=&quot;${escapeText(query)}&quot;&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
 &lt;div class=&quot;grid&quot; id=&quot;units&quot;&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;p class=&quot;muted notice&quot; id=&quot;storageNotice&quot;&gt;${r(storageOK?&#x27;学習記録は、このブラウザに保存されます。&#x27;:&#x27;このブラウザでは学習記録を保存できません。テストはそのまま利用できます。&#x27;)}&lt;/p&gt;`;
 document.getElementById(&#x27;search&#x27;).oninput=e=&gt;{query=e.target.value;cards()};cards();
}
function cards(){
 const term=query.trim().normalize(&#x27;NFKC&#x27;).toLowerCase();
 document.getElementById(&#x27;units&#x27;).innerHTML=DATA.filter(u=&gt;u.domain===domain&amp;&amp;(`${u.n} ${u.name}`).normalize(&#x27;NFKC&#x27;).toLowerCase().includes(term)).map(u=&gt;`&lt;button data-domain=&quot;${u.domain}&quot; class=&quot;unit&quot; onclick=&quot;start(${u.n})&quot;&gt;&lt;span class=&quot;num&quot;&gt;${u.n} / 33&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span class=&quot;course&quot;&gt;${r(&#x27;単元&#x27;)}${u.n}&lt;/span&gt;&lt;b&gt;${u.title}&lt;/b&gt;&lt;small&gt;${history[u.n]?`${history[u.n].score} / 12 · ${r(&#x27;前のスコア&#x27;)}`:&#x27;○× 10 + 5つからえらぶ 2&#x27;}　→&lt;/small&gt;&lt;/button&gt;`).join(&#x27;&#x27;)||&#x27;&lt;p&gt;みつかりませんでした。キーワードをかえてください。&lt;/p&gt;&#x27;;
}
function start(n){const chosen=DATA.find(u=&gt;u.n===n);if(!chosen)return;unit=chosen;domain=unit.domain;answers={};selections={};queue=unit.questions.map((_,i)=&gt;i);idx=0;review=false;show();window.scrollTo(0,0)}
function jump(i){if(i&lt;0||i&gt;=queue.length)return;idx=i;show();window.scrollTo(0,0)}
function show(){
 document.body.dataset.domain=unit.domain;
 const qi=queue[idx],q=unit.questions[qi],answered=answers[qi]!==undefined,chosen=answered?answers[qi]:(selections[qi]||[]),multi=q.select&gt;1,tf=q.options.length===2;
 const done=queue.filter(n=&gt;answers[n]!==undefined).length;
 app.innerHTML=`&lt;button onclick=&quot;home()&quot;&gt;← ${r(&#x27;単元&#x27;)}をえらぶ&lt;/button&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;course-heading muted&quot;&gt;${domains[unit.domain]}&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;toolbar&quot;&gt;&lt;h2 class=&quot;screen-title&quot; tabindex=&quot;-1&quot;&gt;${unit.n}　${unit.title}&lt;/h2&gt;&lt;span&gt;${review?&#x27;もういちど · &#x27;:&#x27;&#x27;}${idx+1} / ${queue.length}&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;progress&quot; role=&quot;progressbar&quot; aria-label=&quot;解答済みの問題数&quot; aria-valuenow=&quot;${done}&quot; aria-valuemin=&quot;0&quot; aria-valuemax=&quot;${queue.length}&quot;&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;width:${done/queue.length*100}%&quot;&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
 &lt;section class=&quot;panel&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;muted&quot;&gt;${qi+1} · ${tf?&#x27;○×&#x27;:`5つから${q.select}つえらぶ`}&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;question&quot;&gt;${q.text}&lt;/div&gt;
 ${multi?`&lt;p class=&quot;multi-note&quot;&gt;${q.select}つえらんで「${r(&#x27;答えを確定する&#x27;)}」をおしてください。&lt;/p&gt;`:&#x27;&#x27;}
 &lt;div class=&quot;options ${tf?&#x27;tf&#x27;:&#x27;&#x27;}&quot;&gt;${q.options.map((s,i)=&gt;{
  const sel=chosen.includes(i),right=q.answer.includes(i);
  const cls=answered?(right?&#x27;correct &#x27;:&#x27;&#x27;)+(sel?(right?&#x27;chosen&#x27;:&#x27;wrong&#x27;):&#x27;&#x27;):(sel?&#x27;selected&#x27;:&#x27;&#x27;);
  const disabled=answered||(multi&amp;&amp;!sel&amp;&amp;chosen.length&gt;=q.select);
  return `&lt;button class=&quot;option ${cls}&quot; ${disabled?&#x27;disabled&#x27;:&#x27;&#x27;} ${multi&amp;&amp;!answered?`aria-pressed=&quot;${sel}&quot;`:&#x27;&#x27;} onclick=&quot;choose(${i})&quot;&gt;${tf?s:`&lt;span class=&quot;answer-mark&quot;&gt;${i+1}.${multi&amp;&amp;sel?&#x27; ✓&#x27;:&#x27;&#x27;}&lt;/span&gt; ${s}`}&lt;/button&gt;`
 }).join(&#x27;&#x27;)}&lt;/div&gt;
 ${multi&amp;&amp;!answered?`&lt;div class=&quot;multi-confirm&quot;&gt;&lt;button class=&quot;primary&quot; onclick=&quot;confirmAnswer()&quot; ${chosen.length!==q.select?&#x27;disabled&#x27;:&#x27;&#x27;}&gt;${r(&#x27;答えを確定する&#x27;)}&lt;/button&gt;&lt;span&gt;${r(&#x27;選択中&#x27;)} ${chosen.length} / ${q.select}&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;`:&#x27;&#x27;}
 &lt;div id=&quot;feedback&quot; aria-live=&quot;polite&quot;&gt;${answered?feedback(q,answers[qi]):&#x27;&#x27;}&lt;/div&gt;
 &lt;div class=&quot;foot&quot;&gt;&lt;button onclick=&quot;jump(${idx-1})&quot; ${idx===0?&#x27;disabled&#x27;:&#x27;&#x27;}&gt;← まえへ&lt;/button&gt;&lt;button class=&quot;primary&quot; ${!answered?&#x27;disabled&#x27;:&#x27;&#x27;} onclick=&quot;next()&quot;&gt;${idx===queue.length-1?r(&#x27;結果&#x27;)+&#x27;をみる&#x27;:&#x27;つぎへ →&#x27;}&lt;/button&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/section&gt;
 &lt;div class=&quot;pills&quot; aria-label=&quot;問題を選ぶ&quot;&gt;${queue.map((n,i)=&gt;`&lt;button class=&quot;${i===idx?&#x27;current &#x27;:&#x27;&#x27;}${answers[n]!==undefined?(same(answers[n],unit.questions[n].answer)?&#x27;done-ok&#x27;:&#x27;done-bad&#x27;):&#x27;&#x27;}&quot; onclick=&quot;jump(${i})&quot; ${i===idx?&#x27;aria-current=&quot;step&quot;&#x27;:&#x27;&#x27;} aria-label=&quot;問題${n+1}${answers[n]!==undefined?(same(answers[n],unit.questions[n].answer)?&#x27; 正解&#x27;:&#x27; 不正解&#x27;):&#x27;&#x27;}&quot;&gt;${n+1}${answers[n]!==undefined?(same(answers[n],unit.questions[n].answer)?&#x27; ✓&#x27;:&#x27; ×&#x27;):&#x27;&#x27;}&lt;/button&gt;`).join(&#x27;&#x27;)}&lt;/div&gt;`;
}
function answerLabel(q,a){return q.options.length===2?q.options[a[0]]:a.slice().sort((x,y)=&gt;x-y).map(i=&gt;i+1).join(&#x27;・&#x27;)}
function feedback(q,a){return `&lt;div class=&quot;feedback ${same(q.answer,a)?&#x27;&#x27;:&#x27;bad&#x27;}&quot;&gt;&lt;strong&gt;${same(q.answer,a)?r(&#x27;正解です！&#x27;):&#x27;もういちど、たしかめましょう。&#x27;}&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;p&gt;${r(&#x27;正解&#x27;)}：${answerLabel(q,q.answer)}&lt;/p&gt;${q.explanation}&lt;span class=&quot;source-ref&quot;&gt;${q.reference}&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;`}
function choose(a){
 const qi=queue[idx],q=unit.questions[qi];if(answers[qi]!==undefined||!Number.isInteger(a)||a&lt;0||a&gt;=q.options.length)return;
 if(q.select===1){answers[qi]=[a];show();return}
 const s=selections[qi]||(selections[qi]=[]),at=s.indexOf(a);if(at&gt;=0)s.splice(at,1);else if(s.length&lt;q.select)s.push(a);show();
}
function confirmAnswer(){const qi=queue[idx],q=unit.questions[qi],s=selections[qi]||[];if(answers[qi]!==undefined||s.length!==q.select)return;answers[qi]=s.slice().sort((a,b)=&gt;a-b);show()}
function next(){if(answers[queue[idx]]===undefined)return;if(idx&lt;queue.length-1)jump(idx+1);else result()}
function result(){
 const unanswered=queue.findIndex(i=&gt;answers[i]===undefined);if(unanswered!==-1){jump(unanswered);return}
 const wrong=queue.filter(i=&gt;!same(answers[i],unit.questions[i].answer)),score=queue.length-wrong.length;
 if(!review){history[unit.n]={score,date:Date.now()};save()}
 app.innerHTML=`&lt;button onclick=&quot;home()&quot;&gt;← ${r(&#x27;単元&#x27;)}をえらぶ&lt;/button&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;panel&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;brand&quot;&gt;LESSON COMPLETE&lt;/div&gt;&lt;h2&gt;${unit.n}　${unit.title}&lt;/h2&gt;&lt;p&gt;${review?&#x27;もういちどの学習、おつかれさまでした。&#x27;:&#x27;おつかれさまでした。&#x27;}&lt;/p&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;score&quot;&gt;${score}&lt;span style=&quot;font-size:24px&quot;&gt; / ${queue.length}&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;p&gt;${wrong.length?&#x27;まちがえたところを、もういちど。ひとつずつ身につけていきましょう。&#x27;:&#x27;すべてできました！ よくがんばりました。&#x27;}&lt;/p&gt;
 &lt;div class=&quot;foot&quot;&gt;${wrong.length?&#x27;&lt;button class=&quot;primary&quot; id=&quot;retry&quot;&gt;まちがえたところだけ&lt;/button&gt;&#x27;:&#x27;&#x27;}&lt;button onclick=&quot;start(${unit.n})&quot;&gt;はじめから&lt;/button&gt;${unit.n&lt;DATA.length?`&lt;button onclick=&quot;start(${unit.n+1})&quot;&gt;つぎの${r(&#x27;単元&#x27;)} →&lt;/button&gt;`:&#x27;&#x27;}&lt;/div&gt;
 &lt;div class=&quot;score-list&quot; aria-label=&quot;解答の結果&quot;&gt;${queue.map((qi,i)=&gt;{const q=unit.questions[qi];return `&lt;button onclick=&quot;jump(${i})&quot;&gt;${qi+1} · ${same(answers[qi],q.answer)?&#x27;✓ &#x27;+r(&#x27;正解&#x27;):&#x27;× &#x27;+r(&#x27;不正解&#x27;)}&lt;small&gt;${r(&#x27;あなたの答え&#x27;)}：${answerLabel(q,answers[qi])} ／ ${r(&#x27;正解&#x27;)}：${answerLabel(q,q.answer)}　${r(&#x27;解説&#x27;)}をみる →&lt;/small&gt;&lt;/button&gt;`}).join(&#x27;&#x27;)}&lt;/div&gt;${!storageOK?&#x27;&lt;p class=&quot;notice&quot;&gt;&#x27;+r(&#x27;このブラウザでは学習記録を保存できませんでした。&#x27;)+&#x27;&lt;/p&gt;&#x27;:&#x27;&#x27;}&lt;/div&gt;`;
 if(wrong.length)document.getElementById(&#x27;retry&#x27;).onclick=()=&gt;{queue=wrong;wrong.forEach(i=&gt;{delete answers[i];delete selections[i]});idx=0;review=true;show();window.scrollTo(0,0)};
 window.scrollTo(0,0);
}
home();
